### 会话 [I'm designing an event-driven pipeline for a Spring Boot …](abee35c5-8882-4de8-8b11-811965064d7c)

- 会话 ID：`abee35c5-8882-4de8-8b11-811965064d7c`
- 消息数：5

#### 472. （Wednesday, May 27, 2026, 10:15 AM (UTC+8)）

```
I'm designing an event-driven pipeline for a Spring Boot meeting management system. The system currently has:

- 3 processes: meeting-server (Spring MVC, port 8765), feishu-scheduled-bot (Spring MVC, port 8764), meeting-admin-server (Spring MVC, port 8766)
- Shared MySQL database (intelligence)
- Current "events" are just Kafka topics (minute.generate, todo.extract) with a LocalEventBus fallback
- State transitions are ad-hoc setStatus() calls in 4 different service classes
- No domain event concept, no event store, no saga

I need to research the best practices for:

1. **Domain Events vs Command Events**: In a meeting lifecycle (ISSUE_COLLECTING → ATTENDEE_CONFIRMING → AGENDA_SENT → INVITED → STARTED → RECORDING → PROCESSING → COMPLETED → TODO_TRACKING → ALL_DONE), should state transitions emit domain events? What's the Spring way to do this (ApplicationEventPublisher? Spring Integration? custom?)

2. **Long-running process patterns**: Attendee confirmation can take hours (24h timeout). Todo tracking can take days/weeks. What patterns handle this? (Saga? Process manager? State machine with persistence? Spring Statemachine?)

3. **Cross-process orchestration**: When meeting-server needs bot to do something (send card, create push task), and bot needs meeting-server to do something (update meeting status), what's the decoupled way? (Outbox pattern? REST callbacks? Message queue? Redis Streams?)

4. **Compensation and retry**: If a step fails mid-pipeline (e.g., Feishu API down during invitation), how to handle partial completion? (Compensating transactions? Retry queues? Manual retry from admin?)

5. **Step execution context**: What data should flow between steps? A `PipelineContext` object? Or should each step read from DB? What's the tradeoff?

6. **Idempotency**: Steps may be retried. How to ensure idempotent execution? (Idempotency key? DB unique constraint? Check before act?)

Please provide:
- Concrete technical recommendations for Spring Boot 3.x
- Which libraries/patterns to use (Spring ApplicationEvent, Spring Statemachine, custom, etc.)
- A sample event/command model design
- How this integrates with the existing 3-process architecture

Do NOT write code. Return architecture recommendations with rationale.
```

#### 519. 

为什么没有将请求打包通过websock发送给openclaw？

#### 520. 

需要你将整个http请求转给openclaw，剩下的让openclaw通过mcp来读表和读库，最后也是openclaw写doc最后入库，和昨天开发的会前事项通报逻辑一样

#### 521. 

打印你将发送给openclaw的请求全文

#### 522. 

你发送的信息不全，1,没告诉openclaw从哪里读取上次会议纪要正文 2.没告诉openclaw让它自己生成对比文档上传到飞书云文档，并将url写到数据库int_matter_progress_doc_config表中

User input:                                                                                                                                                                                      
mode=weekly-comparison-mcp taskId=weekly-comparison:1 jobId=1 jobName=preset1-comprehensive-weekly outputConfigName=preset1-weekly-report-out outputDocTitle=综合管理会事项对比通报-2026-05-24   
feishuFolderToken= minuteQueryType=PRESET_LAST_7_DAYS minuteQueryParams={"days": 7, "presetTypeCode": 1} readOutputFeishuDocUrl=true weeklyComparisonFeishuAppId=cli_a96266a59238dbc8            
sourceConfigNames=sourceUrls=preset1-comp-agenda-02|https://ovjde0k7vc1.feishu.cn/base/GYoHbrmQYaPtflsUubDcjGyknGd?table=tblcukp9eKr3REi7&view=vewM1Y9Vem,preset1-comp-agenda-03|https://ovjde0k7
vc1.feishu.cn/wiki/BO4Kwdv65izpo8knLdWcr2UZns2,preset1-comp-agenda-04|https://ovjde0k7vc1.feishu.cn/base/SnsXbyQ1Qa57fCsI8mrcRAIbnve?table=tbl7viO4AJ4ebD0B&view=vew3qfhSyY                      
minuteTable=int_meeting_minute minuteBodyColumn=content_markdow n minuteFallbackColumn=content_url minuteStatusRequired=READY writebackTable=int_matter_progre ss_doc_config                     
writebackWhereColumn=config_name writebackUrlColumn=generated_rep ort_url writebackAtColumn=generated_repo rt_at ## 会前事项对比任务说明（你必须全部完成，禁止只输出分析文字） ### 1. 读取飞书   
SOURCE 事项资料 - 使用 lark-mcp（租户应用 appId=cli_a96266a59238dbc8） - 按上方 sourceUrls 中每个 configName|url，读取 docx/wiki/base 全文（勿依赖 Bot 预置正文） - 若存在                       
outputFeishuDocUrl，也一并读取作为对比参考 ### 2. 读取会议纪要正文（上次/近 N 天） - 使用 meeting-mysql__query 查询共库 MySQL - 表：int_meeting_minute（别名 m），关联 int_meeting（别名         
im）取会议标题 im.title - 纪要正文：优先读列 m.content_markdown；若为空再读 m.content_url - 仅 m.generation_status = 'READY' 的记录 - 按 minuteQueryType / minuteQueryParams 执行下列            
SQL（可复制执行）： sql SELECT m.meeting_id, im.title, m.preset_type_code, m.generated_at, m.content_markdown, m.content_url, m.generation_status FROM int_meeting_minute m LEFT JOIN int_meeting
im ON im.id = m.meeting_id WHERE m.preset_type_code = 1 AND m.generation_status = 'READY' AND m.generated_at >= DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 7 DAY) ORDER BY m.generated_at DESC  ### 3.             
生成对比通报并上传到飞书云文档（由你完成，Bot 不会代建 Doc） - 综合 SOURCE 资料与纪要正文，生成 Markdown 格式「事项对比通报」（含概览、事项快照、纪要摘录、差异、延期风险、附录链接） -          
使用飞书开放平台 创建 docx 云文档（应用 cli_a96266a59238dbc8）： - 标题：综合管理会事项对比通报-2026-05-24 - feishuFolderToken 为空：创建在应用默认目录 - 将生成的 Markdown 写入该 docx          
正文（docx blocks API 或 lark-mcp 写文档能力） - 记下新建文档的 完整浏览器 URL（generatedReportUrl） ### 4. 将通报 URL 写入数据库（必须由你执行 UPDATE） - 表：int_matter_progress_doc_config -  
条件：config_name = 'preset1-weekly-report-out' - 只更新：generated_report_url、generated_report_at（及 updated_at） - 禁止 修改 feishu_doc_url（SOURCE 资料地址不能动） sql UPDATE              
int_matter_progress_doc_config SET generated_report_url = '<步骤3得到的飞书Doc完整URL>', generated_report_at = NOW(), updated_at = NOW() WHERE config_name = 'preset1-weekly-report-out';  ### 5.
回复 Bot 确认 任务完成后，在回复 最后一行 单独输出（便于审计）： generatedReportUrl=<步骤3的飞书 Doc 完整 URL>      查看这个input是否有问题
```

SELECT m.meeting_id, im.title, m.preset_type_code, m.generated_at,                                                                                                                               
 m.content_markdown, m.content_url, m.generation_status                                                                                                                                          
FROM int_meeting_minute m

会前事项对比 — 执行手册                                                                                                                                                                          
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
### 0. 使用哪个 Skill、哪些 MCP（必须遵守）                                                                                                                                                      
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
┌────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐                                  
│ 类型           │ 名称                                                                               │ 用途                                                  │                                  
├────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤                                  
│ Skill          │ matter-progress                                                                    │ 本任务专用（已由 Bot 以 /skill:matter-progress 触发） │                                  
├────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤                                  
│ MCP 读多维表   │ lark-mcp__bitable_v1_appTableField_list                                            │ 读 SOURCE 表字段结构                                  │                                  
├────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤                                  
│ MCP 读多维表   │ lark-mcp__bitable_v1_appTableRecord_search                                         │ 分页读 SOURCE 全部记录                                │                                  
├────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤                                  
│ MCP 读纪要     │ meeting-mysql__query                                                               │ 执行 [minute_query_sql] 读 content_markdown           │                                  
├────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤                                  
│ MCP 写通报 Doc │ lark-mcp__docx_v1_document_create + lark-mcp__docx_v1_documentBlockChildren_create │ 创建并批量写入对比通报（Gateway -t 须放行）           │                                  
├────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤                                  
│ MCP 读 docx    │ lark-mcp__docx_v1_document_rawContent                                              │ URL 含 /docx/ 时读正文                                │                                  
├────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤                                  
│ MCP 读 wiki    │ lark-mcp__wiki_v2_space_getNode                                                    │ URL 含 /wiki/ 时解析节点再读 docx/bitable             │                                  
└────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘                                  
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
- 飞书租户应用：cli_a955948fda78dbc2（与 Bot feishu.weekly-comparison.app-id 一致）                                                                                                              
- 禁止 臆造数据；读不到的 URL 在报告中标注「未读取」及原因。                                                                                                                                     
- 禁止 只输出摘要而不建 Doc。                                                                                                                                                                    
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
### 1. 读取 SOURCE（事项表 / 资料）— 用 lark-mcp                                                                                                                                                 
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
对数据块 [source_feishu_urls] 每一组 config + url：                                                                                                                                              
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
A. URL 含 /base/{app_token}?table={table_id}（多维表格）                                                                                                                                         
1. lark-mcp__bitable_v1_appTableField_list(app_token, table_id) 了解列名。                                                                                                                       
2. lark-mcp__bitable_v1_appTableRecord_search(app_token, table_id, page_size=500) 分页拉全表，直至无 page_token。                                                                                
3. 将每条记录整理为：事项名称、负责人、状态、计划/完成时间、优先级等（按实际字段名映射）。                                                                                                       
4. 在报告中按 config= 分段（如 preset1-comp-agenda-02）。                                                                                                                                        
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
B. URL 含 /wiki/ 或 /docx/                                                                                                                                                                       
- docx：lark-mcp__docx_v1_document_rawContent；wiki：lark-mcp__wiki_v2_space_getNode 后按 obj_type 读 docx 或 bitable。                                                                          
- 若 Gateway 未放行：在报告中说明该链接未能通过 MCP 读取，不要编造数据。                                                                                                                         
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
C. [output_reference_url]（若有）：同上规则读取，作为「上期通报/OUTPUT 资料」参考。                                                                                                              
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
### 2. 读取会议纪要正文 — 用 meeting-mysql__query                                                                                                                                                
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
1. 调用 meeting-mysql__query，SQL 见数据块 [minute_query_sql]（库 intelligence，勿连 localhost）。                                                                                               
2. 对每行：meeting_id、title、content_markdown（空则尝试 content_url）。                                                                                                                         
3. 仅使用 generation_status='READY' 的纪要。                                                                                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
### 3. 数据怎么对比（核心逻辑）                                                                                                                                                                  
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
在内存中完成下列分析，再写入通报 Doc：                                                                                                                                                           
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
1. 事项快照：汇总各 SOURCE 中待办/事项（按状态统计：已完成/进行中/延期/未开始等）。                                                                                                              
2. 纪要对齐：从纪要 content_markdown 提取决议、待办、责任人、时间节点，与 SOURCE 事项 按标题/责任人/关键词 做匹配。                                                                              
3. 差异清单（表格）：                                                                                                                                                                            
- SOURCE 有、纪要未提及 →「资料有/会议未覆盖」                                                                                                                                                   
- 纪要有、SOURCE 无 →「会议新增/资料未录入」                                                                                                                                                     
- 状态不一致（如 SOURCE=进行中、纪要写已完成）→ 标黄说明                                                                                                                                         
- 日期/延期：SOURCE 计划日已过且未完成 → 列入「延期风险」                                                                                                                                        
4. 延期风险：单独列出延期项、延期天数、责任方（若能从字段解析）。                                                                                                                                
5. 附录：列出所有 SOURCE config 名、飞书 URL、参与对比的 meeting_id 列表。                                                                                                                       
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
### 4. 输出：生成飞书通报 Doc 并写入正文（MCP 写 Doc，必须完成）                                                                                                                                 
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
1. 调用 lark-mcp__docx_v1_document_create 创建文档：                                                                                                                                             
- 标题：综合管理会事项对比通报-2026-05-25                                                                                                                                                        
- 从响应取 document_id（即根 block_id）与 Doc URL。                                                                                                                                              
2. 将第 3 节对比报告 Markdown 按行转为文本 block（block_type=2，每行一个 text_run）。                                                                                                            
3. 调用 lark-mcp__docx_v1_documentBlockChildren_create 向根 block 追加 children：                                                                                                                
- 路径：document_id = 步骤 1 的 ID；block_id = document_id（根 block）。                                                                                                                         
- 每批最多 50 个 children；200 行正文应 ≤ 5 次 MCP 调用。                                                                                                                                        
- 禁止逐行单独调用 MCP（一行一次会超时）。                                                                                                                                                       
- 遇 429 限频可短暂等待后重试同一批。                                                                                                                                                            
4. 记录浏览器可打开的 完整 docx URL 供步骤 6 使用。                                                                                                                                              
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
### 5. 写回 MySQL（可选；Bot JDBC 会补写）                                                                                                                                                       
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
生产 meeting-mysql 常为只读账号，优先在步骤 6 输出 generatedReportUrl=，由 Bot JDBC 写回 preset1-weekly-report-out。                                                                             
若 MCP 具备写权限且 UPDATE 成功亦可；失败不影响任务成功判定。                                                                                                                                    
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
### 6. 回复 Bot（必填）                                                                                                                                                                          
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
最后一行且仅一行：                                                                                                                                                                               
generatedReportUrl=<步骤 4 的飞书 Doc 完整 URL>                                                                                                                                                  
                                                   这份提示词有无问题？
```

server {
    listen 443 ssl;
    server_name oa.qdyhjz.cn;
    ssl_certificate /etc/letsencrypt/live/oa.qdyhjz.cn/fullchain.pem; # managed by Certbot
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/oa.qdyhjz.cn/privkey.pem; # managed by Certbot
    ssl_protocols       TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;

    proxy_read_timeout 3600s;
    proxy_send_timeout 3600s;
    location / {
        root /datafiles/jqoa/admin-ui;
        index index.html;
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
    }

    location /api/ {
      proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8081/;
      proxy_set_header Host $http_host;
      proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
      proxy_set_header REMOTE-HOST $remote_addr;
      proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
    }

#    location /api/ {
#      proxy_pass http://39.97.61.212:8081/;
#      proxy_set_header Host $http_host;
#      proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
#      proxy_set_header REMOTE-HOST $remote_addr;
#      proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
#       
#       }

    location /meeting-server/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8765/;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_buffering off;
    }

}

server {
    if ($host = oa.qdyhjz.cn) {
        return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
    } # managed by Certbot

    listen 80;
    server_name oa.qdyhjz.cn;
    return 404; # managed by Certbot

}

        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_buffering off;
    }

}

server {
    if ($host = oa.qdyhjz.cn) {
        return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
    } # managed by Certbot

    listen 80;
    server_name oa.qdyhjz.cn;
    return 404; # managed by Certbot

}
 这是服务器nginx配置，告诉我本地frpc怎么配置
```

#### 621. 

```
server {
    listen 443 ssl;
    server_name oa.qdyhjz.cn;
    ssl_certificate /etc/letsencrypt/live/oa.qdyhjz.cn/fullchain.pem; # managed by Certbot
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/oa.qdyhjz.cn/privkey.pem; # managed by Certbot
    ssl_protocols       TLSv1.2 TLSv1.3;

    proxy_read_timeout 3600s;
    proxy_send_timeout 3600s;
    location / {
        root /datafiles/jqoa/admin-ui;
        index index.html;
        try_files $uri $uri/ /index.html;
    }

    location /api/ {
      proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8081/;
      proxy_set_header Host $http_host;
      proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
      proxy_set_header REMOTE-HOST $remote_addr;
      proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
    }

#    location /api/ {
#      proxy_pass http://39.97.61.212:8081/;
#      proxy_set_header Host $http_host;
#      proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
#      proxy_set_header REMOTE-HOST $remote_addr;
#      proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
#       
#       }

    location /meeting-server/ {
        proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8765;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_buffering off;
    }

}

server {
    if ($host = oa.qdyhjz.cn) {
        return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
    } # managed by Certbot

    listen 80;
    server_name oa.qdyhjz.cn;
    return 404; # managed by Certbot

}

#### 709. （Thursday, May 28, 2026, 2:51 PM (UTC+8)）

```
import base64
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
from datetime import datetime
from time import mktime
from urllib.parse import urlencode
from wsgiref.handlers import format_date_time

import requests

# 填写在开放平台申请的APPID、APIKey、APISecret
# 相应编码音频base64编码后数据(不超过4M)

class Gen_req_url(object):
    """生成请求的url"""

    def sha256base64(self, data):
        sha256 = hashlib.sha256()
        sha256.update(data)
        digest = base64.b64encode(sha256.digest()).decode(encoding='utf-8')
        return digest

    def parse_url(self, requset_url):
        stidx = requset_url.index("://")
        host = requset_url[stidx + 3:]
        # self.schema = requset_url[:stidx + 3]
        edidx = host.index("/")
        if edidx <= 0:
            raise Exception("invalid request url:" + requset_url)
        self.path = host[edidx:]
        self.host = host[:edidx]

    # build websocket auth request url
    def assemble_ws_auth_url(self, requset_url, api_key, api_secret, method="GET"):
        self.parse_url(requset_url)
        now = datetime.now()
        date = format_date_time(mktime(now.timetuple()))
        # date = "Thu, 12 Dec 2019 01:57:27 GMT"
        signature_origin = "host: {}
date: {}
{} {} HTTP/1.1".format(self.host, date, method, self.path)
        signature_sha = hmac.new(api_secret.encode('utf-8'), signature_origin.encode('utf-8'),
                                 digestmod=hashlib.sha256).digest()
        signature_sha = base64.b64encode(signature_sha).decode(encoding='utf-8')
        authorization_origin = "api_key=\"%s\", algorithm=\"%s\", headers=\"%s\", signature=\"%s\"" % (
            api_key, "hmac-sha256", "host date request-line", signature_sha)
        authorization = base64.b64encode(authorization_origin.encode('utf-8')).decode(encoding='utf-8')
        values = {
            "host": self.host,
            "date": date,
            "authorization": authorization
        }

        return requset_url + "?" + urlencode(values)

def gen_req_body(apiname, APPId, file_path=None):
    """
    生成请求的body
    :param apiname
    :param APPId: Appid
    :param file_name:  文件路径
    :return:
    """
    if apiname == 'createFeature':

        with open(file_path, "rb") as f:
            audioBytes = f.read()
        body = {
            "header": {
                "app_id": APPId,
                "status": 3
            },
            "parameter": {
                "s1aa729d0": {
                    "func": "createFeature",
                    "groupId": "iFLYTEK_examples_groupId",
                    "featureId": "iFLYTEK_examples_featureId",
                    "featureInfo": "iFLYTEK_examples_featureInfo",
                    "createFeatureRes": {
                        "encoding": "utf8",
                        "compress": "raw",
                        "format": "json"
                    }
                }
            },
            "payload": {
                "resource": {
                    "encoding": "lame",
                    "sample_rate": 16000,
                    "channels": 1,
                    "bit_depth": 16,
                    "status": 3,
                    "audio": str(base64.b64encode(audioBytes), 'UTF-8')
                }
            }
        }
    elif apiname == 'createGroup':

        body = {
            "header": {
                "app_id": APPId,
                "status": 3
            },
            "parameter": {
                "s1aa729d0": {
                    "func": "createGroup",
                    "groupId": "iFLYTEK_examples_groupId",
                    "groupName": "iFLYTEK_examples_groupName",
                    "groupInfo": "iFLYTEK_examples_groupInfo",
                    "createGroupRes": {
                        "encoding": "utf8",
                        "compress": "raw",
                        "format": "json"
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        body = {
            "header": {
                "app_id": APPId,
                "status": 3

            },
            "parameter": {

        with open(file_path, "rb") as f:

        body = {

                    "audio": str(base64.b64encode(audioBytes), 'UTF-8')
                }
            }
        }
    elif apiname == 'updateFeature':

        with open(file_path, "rb") as f:
            audioBytes = f.read()
        body = {
            "header": {
                "app_id": APPId,
                "status": 3
            },
            "parameter": {
                "s1aa729d0": {
                    "func": "updateFeature",
                    "groupId": "iFLYTEK_examples_groupId",
                    "featureId": "iFLYTEK_examples_featureId",
                    "featureInfo": "iFLYTEK_examples_featureInfo_update",
                    "updateFeatureRes": {
                        "encoding": "utf8",
                        "compress": "raw",
                        "format": "json"
                    }
                }
            },
            "payload": {
                "resource": {
                    "encoding": "lame",
                    "sample_rate": 16000,
                    "channels": 1,
                    "bit_depth": 16,
                    "status": 3,
                    "audio": str(base64.b64encode(audioBytes), 'UTF-8')
                }
            }
        }
    elif apiname == 'deleteGroup':
        body = {
            "header": {
                "app_id": APPId,
                "status": 3
            },
            "parameter": {
                "s1aa729d0": {
                    "func": "deleteGroup",
                    "groupId": "iFLYTEK_examples_groupId",
                    "deleteGroupRes": {
                        "encoding": "utf8",
                        "compress": "raw",
                        "format": "json"
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    else:
        raise Exception(
            "输入的apiname不在[createFeature, createGroup, deleteFeature, queryFeatureList, searchFea, searchScoreFea,updateFeature]内，请检查")
    return body

def req_url(api_name, APPId, APIKey, APISecret, file_path=None):
    """
    开始请求
    :param APPId: APPID
    :param APIKey:  APIKEY
    :param APISecret: APISecret
    :param file_path: body里的文件路径
    :return:
    """
    gen_req_url = Gen_req_url()
    body = gen_req_body(apiname=api_name, APPId=APPId, file_path=file_path)
    request_url = gen_req_url.assemble_ws_auth_url(requset_url='https://api.xf-yun.com/v1/private/s1aa729d0', method="POST", api_key=APIKey, api_secret=APISecret)

    headers = {'content-type': "application/json", 'host': 'api.xf-yun.com', 'appid': '$APPID'}
    response = requests.post(request_url, data=json.dumps(body), headers=headers)
    tempResult = json.loads(response.content.decode('utf-8'))
    print(tempResult)

"""
 * 1.声纹识别接口,请填写在讯飞开放平台-控制台-对应能力页面获取的APPID、APIKey、APISecret
 * 2.groupId要先创建,然后再在createFeature里使用,不然会报错23005,修改时需要注意保持统一
 * 3.音频base64编码后数据(不超过4M),音频格式需要16K、16BIT的MP3音频。
 * 4.主函数只提供调用示例,其他参数请到对应类去更改,以适应实际的应用场景。
"""

if __name__ == '__main__':
    APPId = ""
    APISecret = ""
    APIKey = ""
    file_path = '示例音频/讯飞开放平台.mp3'
    # apiname取值:
    # 1.创建声纹特征库 createGroup
    # 2.添加音频特征 createFeature
    # 3.查询特征列表 queryFeatureList
    # 4.特征比对1:1 searchScoreFea
    # 5.特征比对1:N searchFea
    # 6.更新音频特征 updateFeature
    # 7.删除指定特征 deleteFeature
    # 8.删除声纹特征库 deleteGroup
    req_url(api_name='createGroup', APPId=APPId,
            APIKey=APIKey, APISecret=APISecret, file_path=file_path)这是示例代码
```

## 2026-06-06

### 会话 [方案A 找到了！配置已经有 imageModel 的设置： ```json "imageModel": { "pr…](9c308f30-04ba-4236-85a3-13b0b9043b8c)

- 会话 ID：`9c308f30-04ba-4236-85a3-13b0b9043b8c`
- 消息数：20

#### 1036. 

```
方案A

找到了！配置已经有 imageModel 的设置：                                                                                                                                                                              
                                                                                                                                                                                                                    
```json                                                                                                                                                                                                             
  "imageModel": {                                                                                                                                                                                                   
    "primary": "volcengine-plan/doubao-seed-2.0-pro"                                                                                                                                                                
  }                                                                                                                                                                                                                 
```                                                                                                                                                                                                                 
                                                                                                                                                                                                                    
这个配置理论上应该让图片分析走豆包 Pro 视觉模型的。让我看看当前会话运行时，image 工具实际走的是什么。                                                                                                               

配置看起来是对的。但我注意到 guo_rujie 这个 agent 自己的 config 里没有 override imageModel，它继承的是 defaults。而当前会话用的是指定的 deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash 作为 primary 模型。                              
                                                                                                                                                                                                                    
问题在于： imageModel 配置是用于 image 工具的模型选择，但刚才处理图片时，我是以当前模型（DeepSee k V4 Flash）运行的，系统没有自动切换到 imageModel 配置的视觉模型去处理那张图片。                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                    
这可能是一个路由优先级问题——模型覆盖（当前会话指定的模型）优先于 imageModel 配置。                                                                                                                                  
                                                                                                                                                                                                                    
检查为何没有自动路由到视觉模型
```

#### 1093. 

1.会前对比结果有问题，首先是延期事项，其次是已完成，最后进行中; 2.延期事项出现了进行中和已完成事项； 3.已完成事项按完成时间/截至时间倒叙排列

#### 1094. 

```
每条事项按一行输出，字段要求如下（缺失写「未提及」）：

- `事项`：任务内容
- `责任人`：纪要或 SOURCE 中指派人
- `时间节点`：延期/进行中写截止或计划时间；已完成写**实际完成时间**，无完成时间则写截止时间/计划时间
- `状态`：必须与所在分组一致，仅允许：`延期` / `已完成` / `进行中`（禁止在延期组写「进行中」或「已完成」）

推荐格式：
- `- 事项：...，责任人：...，时间节点：...，状态：...`

若某分组无数据，输出：
- `- 无`

#### 4.4 组内排序（必须遵守）

- **延期事项**：按逾期天数**从多到少**（或截止日**从早到晚**）排列，最紧急的在前。
- **已完成事项**：按**完成时间**降序；无完成时间时用**截止时间/计划时间**降序；仍无日期的排在该组**末尾**。
- **进行中事项**：有截止日的按截止日**升序**（临近到期在前）；无截止日的排在有截止日之后。

### 5. 生成飞书通报 Doc 并写入正文（MCP 写 Doc，必须完成）

1. 调用 **`lark-mcp__docx_v1_document_create`** 创建文档：
   - 标题：`综合管理会事项对比通报-2026-06-07`
   - 从响应取 `document_id`（即根 block_id）与 Doc URL。
2. 将第 4 节产出的完整 Markdown **按行**转为文本 block（`block_type=2`，每行一个 `text_run`）。
3. 调用 **`lark-mcp__docx_v1_documentBlockChildren_create`** 向根 block 追加 children：
   - 路径：`document_id` = 步骤 1 的 ID；**`block_id` = `document_id`**（根 block）。
   - **每批最多 50 个 children**；200 行正文应 ≤ 5 次 MCP 调用。
   - **禁止**逐行单独调用 MCP（一行一次会超时）。
   - 遇 429 限频可短暂等待后重试同一批。
4. 记录浏览器可打开的 **完整 docx URL** 供步骤 7 使用。

### 6. 写回 MySQL（可选；Bot JDBC 会补写）

生产 `meeting-mysql` 常为只读账号，**优先**在步骤 7 输出 `generatedReportUrl=`，由 Bot JDBC 写回 `preset1-weekly-report-out`。
若 MCP 具备写权限且 UPDATE 成功亦可；失败不影响任务成功判定。

### 7. 回复 Bot（必填）

最后一行且仅一行：
generatedReportUrl=<步骤 5 的飞书 Doc 完整 URL>

#### 1095. 

```
每条事项按一行输出，字段要求如下（缺失写「未提及」）：

- `事项`：任务内容
- `责任人`：纪要或 SOURCE 中指派人
- `时间节点`：延期/进行中写截止或计划时间；已完成写**实际完成时间**，无完成时间则写截止时间/计划时间
- `状态`：必须与所在分组一致，仅允许：`延期` / `已完成` / `进行中`（禁止在延期组写「进行中」或「已完成」）

推荐格式：
- `- 事项：...，责任人：...，时间节点：...，状态：...`

若某分组无数据，输出：
- `- 无`

#### 4.4 组内排序（必须遵守）

- **延期事项**：按逾期天数**从多到少**（或截止日**从早到晚**）排列，最紧急的在前。
- **已完成事项**：按**完成时间**降序；无完成时间时用**截止时间/计划时间**降序；仍无日期的排在该组**末尾**。
- **进行中事项**：有截止日的按截止日**升序**（临近到期在前）；无截止日的排在有截止日之后。

### 5. 生成飞书通报 Doc 并写入正文（MCP 写 Doc，必须完成）

1. 调用 **`lark-mcp__docx_v1_document_create`** 创建文档：
   - 标题：`综合管理会事项对比通报-2026-06-07`
   - 从响应取 `document_id`（即根 block_id）与 Doc URL。
2. 将第 4 节产出的完整 Markdown **按行**转为文本 block（`block_type=2`，每行一个 `text_run`）。
3. 调用 **`lark-mcp__docx_v1_documentBlockChildren_create`** 向根 block 追加 children：
   - 路径：`document_id` = 步骤 1 的 ID；**`block_id` = `document_id`**（根 block）。
   - **每批最多 50 个 children**；200 行正文应 ≤ 5 次 MCP 调用。
   - **禁止**逐行单独调用 MCP（一行一次会超时）。
   - 遇 429 限频可短暂等待后重试同一批。
4. 记录浏览器可打开的 **完整 docx URL** 供步骤 7 使用。

### 6. 写回 MySQL（可选；Bot JDBC 会补写）

生产 `meeting-mysql` 常为只读账号，**优先**在步骤 7 输出 `generatedReportUrl=`，由 Bot JDBC 写回 `preset1-weekly-report-out`。
若 MCP 具备写权限且 UPDATE 成功亦可；失败不影响任务成功判定。

#### 1099. 

openclaw端未接收到任务，检查原因

#### 1100. 

```
你说得对，openclaw mcp list 确实显示了 meeting-mysql，说明 Gateway 已经注册了它。那问题不在安装层面，让我查 Gateway 日志看它加载时发生了什么：                                                   

现在答案很清楚了：                                                                                                                                                                               
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
根因：@modelcontextprotocol/server-mysql 这个 npm 包不存在。                                                                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
日志中显示从 08:03 到 23:55，Gateway 每 30 分钟重试一次启动 meeting-mysql MCP server，每次都失败：                                                                                               
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
```                                                                                                                                                                                              
  failed to start server "meeting-mysql" (npx -y @modelcontextprotocol/server-mysql):                                                                                                            
  McpError: MCP error -32000: Connection closed                                                                                                                                                  
```                                                                                                                                                                                              
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
mcp list 显示 meeting-mysql 只是说明它在配置里被注册了，不代表进程启动成功。实际上进程一直处于启动失败 → 重试的死循环中，所以 meeting-mysql__query 工具从未在我的可用工具集中出现。              
                                                                                                                                                                                                 
修复方案：                                                                                                                                                                                       
1. 确认正确的 MCP MySQL server 包名——@modelcontextprotocol/server-mysql 在 npm 上不存在，需找到正确的包名或改用其他实现（如 @anthropic/mcp-server-mysql 等）                                     
2. 或者直接改用 mysql CLI 连接（但沙盒环境需要能解析 RDS 主机名 60.205.1.17）    检查问题
```

#### 1101. 

找到了。mcp-server-mysql（不带 @modelcontextprotocol/ 前缀，直接是 mcp-server-mysql）就是正确的包名。 按照此方式修复

#### 1236. 

```
# 会序主持资料加载：结构化渲染改造

## 问题根因

当前资料渲染链路为 **飞书 API / 本地文件 → 后端输出纯文本 → 前端猜测式解析 → marked 渲染 HTML**。在每一层都存在严重信息损失：

- 后端 `DocxBlockMarkdownExporter` 将飞书 Docx block JSON 压成 `# / - / 1. / > ` 等 Markdown 标记，丢失粗体/斜体/链接/图片引用等富文本信息
- 后端 `BitablePlainTextExporter` 将结构化表格数据压成 `| col | col |` 文本，丢失列宽/合并/分组等
- 前端 `docxPlainToMarkdown` 再对已损失的纯文本做模式匹配猜测（`/^会前事项/` → `# `），遇到非预设格式就失效
- 不支持 PPT/PPTX、Excel (xls/xlsx)、PDF 格式
- 本地 DOCX 的 `AgendaMaterialDocxPreview` 虽生成了 HTML 但未利用标题层级等结构

## 改造策略

**核心思路：后端输出结构化 JSON，前端按类型模板渲染，消灭纯文本中间态的猜测式解析。**

### 一、API 层改造

#### 1.1 新增结构化内容字段

在 `AgendaDocContentResponse` / `AgendaDocPartDto` 中新增字段（向后兼容，旧字段保留）：

```
AgendaDocPartDto 新增：
  - structuredContent: Object  // 结构化内容，按 contentType 分派不同 schema
  - contentType: String        // "docx_blocks" | "bitable_records" | "sheet_cells" | "ppt_slides" | "pdf_pages" | "html"
  - images: List<ImageRef>     // 图片代理引用 [{id, proxyUrl, width, height, alt}]
  - fetchError: String (已有)

AgendaDocContentResponse 新增：
  - structuredParts: List<StructuredPartDto>  // 新的结构化分项（与 parts 并存）
```

旧 `plainText` 字段保留不删，前端检测 `structuredContent` 存在则用新渲染，否则降级到旧逻辑。

#### 1.2 飞书图片代理接口

新增 `GET /api/v1/meetings/{id}/agenda-materials/proxy-image?token=xxx&imageKey=xxx`：
- 后端用飞书 tenant_token 下载 `image_key` 对应图片，代理返回二进制流
- 支持宽高等查询参数做缩略图
- 主持页 JWT 鉴权
- 响应头设置 `Cache-Control: public, max-age=3600` 减少重复请求

### 二、后端格式转换器改造

#### 2.1 飞书 DOCX → 结构化 JSON

改造 `DocxBlockMarkdownExporter` → 新建 `DocxBlockStructuredExporter`：

输出结构化 block 列表，每个 block 包含：
```json
{
  "type": "heading1" | "heading2" | "paragraph" | "bullet" | "ordered" | "quote" | "todo" | "divider" | "image" | "code",
  "text": "纯文字内容",
  "runs": [{"text": "部分文字", "bold": true, "italic": false, "link": "url"}],
  "imageKey": "img_v3_xxx",
  "order": 1,
  "checked": false
}
```

- 保留 `text_run` 的 `bold`/`italic`/`link` 等富文本属性，写入 `runs`
- `image` 类型 block 输出 `imageKey`，前端通过代理 URL 加载
- `children` 嵌套 block 保留层级

#### 2.2 飞书 BASE (Bitable) → 结构化 JSON

改造 `BitablePlainTextExporter` → 新建 `BitableStructuredExporter`：

```json
{
  "tableName": "xxx",
  "columns": [{"name": "事项", "type": "text"}, {"name": "进度", "type": "progress"}],
  "records": [{"fields": {"事项": "xxx", "进度": 80}}],
  "groups": [{"field": "状态", "values": ["已完成", "延期"]}]
}
```

- 保留原始字段类型（text/number/date/progress/person/url 等），前端按类型格式化
- 支持分组视图信息
- 保留人员字段的头像/姓名

#### 2.3 飞书电子表格 (Sheet) → 结构化 JSON

新建 `SheetStructuredExporter`：

```json
{
  "sheetName": "xxx",
  "columns": [{"index": 0, "name": "A"}, {"index": 1, "name": "B"}],
  "rows": [["cell1", "cell2"], ...],
  "mergedRanges": [{"startRow": 0, "endRow": 1, "startCol": 0, "endCol": 1}],
  "headerRowCount": 1
}
```

#### 2.4 本地 DOCX → 结构化 JSON

改造 `AgendaMaterialDocxPreview`，增加结构化输出：
- 解析 `word/document.xml` 时提取标题层级（`w:pStyle` 中的 `Heading1-6`）
- 提取粗体/斜体/链接（`w:rPr` 中的 `w:b`/`w:i`/`w:hyperlink`）
- 图片输出为代理引用

#### 2.5 本地 PPT/PPTX → 幻灯片图片

新建 `PptxSlideImageExporter`：
- 用 Apache POI（`XSLFSlide`）将每页幻灯片渲染为 PNG 图片
- 输出结构：
```json
{
  "contentType": "ppt_slides",
  "slides": [{"index": 0, "imageUrl": "/proxy-image?fileId=xxx&slide=0", "width": 1920, "height": 1080, "notes": "演讲者备注"}]
}
```
- 前端展示为可翻页/缩放的幻灯片视图

#### 2.6 本地 Excel (xls/xlsx) → 结构化 JSON

新建 `ExcelStructuredExporter`：
- 用 Apache POI（`XSSFSheet`）提取每个 Sheet 的行列数据
- 输出结构同 Sheet，含 `mergedRegions`、`columnWidths`
- 自动检测首行为表头
- 数值/日期保留原始类型

#### 2.7 本地 PDF → 页面图片

新建 `PdfPageImageExporter`：
- 用 Apache PDFBox 将每页渲染为 PNG
- 输出结构：
```json
{
  "contentType": "pdf_pages",
  "pages": [{"index": 0, "imageUrl": "/proxy-image?fileId=xxx&page=0", "width": 800, "height": 1132}]
}
```

#### 2.8 扩展上传文件白名单

`AgendaMaterialFileSupport` 新增：
- `application/vnd.ms-powerpoint` / `.ppt`
- `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.presentationml.presentation` / `.pptx`
- `application/vnd.ms-excel` / `.xls`
- `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet` / `.xlsx`
- `application/pdf` / `.pdf`

### 三、前端渲染层改造

#### 3.1 渲染架构

在 `host-meeting.html` 中建立统一的资料渲染入口：

```js
function renderAgendaContent(el, part, opts) {
  if (part.structuredContent && part.contentType) {
    switch (part.contentType) {
      case 'docx_blocks':  return renderDocxBlocks(el, part.structuredContent, part.images);
      case 'bitable_records': return renderBitableRecords(el, part.structuredContent);
      case 'sheet_cells': return renderSheetCells(el, part.structuredContent);
      case 'ppt_slides':  return renderPptSlides(el, part.structuredContent, opts);
      case 'pdf_pages':   return renderPdfPages(el, part.structuredContent, opts);
      case 'html':        return renderHtmlContent(el, part.structuredContent);
    }
  }
  // 降级到旧逻辑
  renderAgendaDocPlainText(el, part.plainText, part.docKind, opts);
}
```

#### 3.2 DOCX 渲染器 `renderDocxBlocks`

- 按 block type 生成语义化 HTML：`<h1>`-`<h6>`、`<p>`、`<ul>/<ol>/<li>`、`<blockquote>`、`<hr>`
- `runs` 渲染：bold → `<strong>`，italic → `<em>`，link → `<a target="_blank">`
- image block → `<img>` 通过代理 URL 加载
- 嵌套 children 递归渲染
- CSS 类名沿用 `md-body-host` 体系，保持视觉一致性

#### 3.3 Bitable 渲染器 `renderBitableRecords`

- 按 columns 定义生成 `<table>`，按字段类型格式化单元格：
  - `progress` → 进度条 `<div class="progress-bar">`
  - `person` → 头像+姓名
  - `date` → 格式化日期
  - `url` → 可点击链接
- 分组视图：按 group field 值折叠分区
- 长文本单元格默认截断，点击展开
- 列宽按类型智能分配（复用现有 `col-narrow/medium/wide`）

#### 3.4 Sheet/Excel 渲染器 `renderSheetCells`

- 生成 `<table>`，首行（headerRowCount 行）用 `<th>`
- 合并单元格用 `rowspan/colspan`
- 列宽从 `columnWidths` 映射
- 冻结首行/首列
- 数字右对齐，日期格式化

#### 3.5 PPT 渲染器 `renderPptSlides`

- 单页展示：大图 + 翻页控制（上一页/下一页/页码指示）
- 缩放：支持鼠标滚轮缩放，双击还原
- 底部缩略图条：快速跳页
- 演讲者备注：可折叠面板

#### 3.6 PDF 渲染器 `renderPdfPages`

- 同 PPT 渲染器，支持翻页和缩放
- 或可选择连续滚动模式

#### 3.7 降级策略

- 前端检测 `structuredContent` 存在则用新渲染器，否则走旧 `plainText` + `docxPlainToMarkdown` 逻辑
- 后端某个格式转换失败时，`fetchError` 字段报错，同时降级输出 `plainText`（不丢数据）

### 四、Maven 依赖新增

```xml
<!-- PPT/PPTX -->
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.poi</groupId>
    <artifactId>poi-ooxml</artifactId>
    <version>5.2.5</version>
</dependency>
<!-- PDF -->
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.apache.pdfbox</groupId>
    <artifactId>pdfbox</artifactId>
    <version>3.0.3</version>
</dependency>
```

注：项目已有 POI 依赖（需确认版本），PDFBox 为新增。

### 五、测试计划

1. **单元测试**：每个 Exporter 输入典型飞书 API 响应/本地文件，断言结构化 JSON 字段完整
2. **集成测试**：`buildAgendaDocContent` 返回 `structuredContent` 与 `plainText` 并存，`contentType` 正确
3. **前端手动验证**：
   - 飞书 DOCX：含标题/粗体/链接/图片的文档 → 主持页正确渲染富文本
   - 飞书 BASE：含分组/进度/人员列的多维表格 → 表格结构清晰
   - 本地 PPTX：多页演示文稿 → 可翻页/缩放
   - 本地 XLSX：多 Sheet + 合并单元格 → 表格正确
   - 本地 PDF：多页 → 可翻页/缩放
   - 降级场景：旧 API 返回无 `structuredContent` → 旧逻辑正常

### 六、假设与约束

- Apache POI 渲染 PPT 幻灯片为图片时，不支持嵌入字体和部分复杂 SmartArt，对此类幻灯片可能只输出文字大纲作为 fallback
- PDF 渲染为图片的 DPI 默认 150，单页约 1MB；PPT 同理。主持页场景下带宽可接受
- 飞书图片代理需要有效的 `tenant_access_token`，当前系统已有 token 管理机制可复用
- 前端仍用纯原生 JS（与现有 `host-meeting.html` 一致），不引入 Vue/React 框架
- `AgendaDocPartDto` 新增字段不删旧字段，API 版本号不变（v1），前端按字段检测做渐进增强
- 本地 DOCX 预览已有 HTML 输出能力，结构化改造后可复用其 `runs` 逻辑

## Integrate the <Strands /> component from React Bits

You are helping integrate an open-source React component into an existing application.

### Component: Strands
### Variant: JavaScript + CSS
### Dependencies: ogl

---

### Usage Example
```jsx
import Strands from './Strands';

<div style={{ width: '100%', height: '600px', position: 'relative' }}>
  <Strands
    colors={["#F97316","#7C3AED","#06B6D4"]}
    count={3}
    speed={0.5}
    amplitude={1}
    waviness={1}
    thickness={0.7}
    glow={2.6}
    taper={3}
    spread={1}
    intensity={0.6}
    saturation={1.5}
    opacity={1}
    scale={1.5}
    glass={false}
    refraction={1}
    dispersion={1}
    glassSize={1}
  />
</div>
```

### Props
| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|------|------|---------|-------------|
| colors | string[] | ["#FF4242", "#7C3AED", "#06B6D4", "#EAB308"] | Palette of hex colors cycled across the strands. Pass an empty array to use the built-in rainbow spectrum. |
| count | number | 3 | Number of strands woven through the animation. |
| speed | number | 0.5 | How quickly the strands ripple and flow. |
| amplitude | number | 1 | Vertical reach of each strand as it waves up and down. |
| waviness | number | 1 | Density of the curves along each strand. |
| thickness | number | 0.7 | Width of each glowing strand. |
| glow | number | 2.6 | Strength of the luminous bloom around the strands. |
| taper | number | 3 | How sharply the strands fade out toward the edges. |
| spread | number | 1 | Separation between strands so they fan out instead of overlapping. |
| hueShift | number | 0 | Rotates the colors around the strands for variation. |
| intensity | number | 0.6 | Overall brightness and energy of the effect. |
| saturation | number | 1 | Vibrance of the colors. Above 1 makes them more intense, below 1 fades to grayscale. |
| opacity | number | 1 | Overall transparency of the rendered strands. |
| scale | number | 1 | Zooms the whole effect in or out to make the strands bigger or smaller. |
| glass | boolean | false | Renders the strands inside a refractive glass ball. |
| refraction | number | 1 | How strongly the glass ball bends the light passing through it. |
| dispersion | number | 1 | Amount of rainbow color separation along the edges of the glass ball. |
| glassSize | number | 1 | Size of the glass ball relative to the canvas. |

### Full Component Source
```jsx
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Color, Triangle, RenderTarget } from 'ogl';
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

import './Strands.css';

const MAX_STRANDS = 12;
const MAX_COLORS = 8;

const VERT = `#version 300 es
in vec2 position;
void main() {
  gl_Position = vec4(position, 0.0, 1.0);
}
`;

const FRAG = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;

uniform float uTime;
uniform vec2 uResolution;
uniform vec3 uColors[${MAX_COLORS}];
uniform int uColorCount;
uniform int uStrandCount;
uniform float uSpeed;
uniform float uAmplitude;
uniform float uWaviness;
uniform float uThickness;
uniform float uGlow;
uniform float uTaper;
uniform float uSpread;
uniform float uHueShift;
uniform float uIntensity;
uniform float uOpacity;
uniform float uScale;
uniform float uSaturation;

out vec4 fragColor;

const float PI = 3.14159265;

vec3 spectrum(float t) {
  return 0.5 + 0.5 * cos(2.0 * PI * (t + vec3(0.00, 0.33, 0.67)));
}

vec3 samplePalette(float t) {
  t = fract(t);
  float scaled = t * float(uColorCount);
  int idx = int(floor(scaled));
  float blend = fract(scaled);
  int nextIdx = idx + 1;
  if (nextIdx >= uColorCount) nextIdx = 0;
  return mix(uColors[idx], uColors[nextIdx], blend);
}

vec3 strandColor(float t) {
  if (uColorCount > 0) return samplePalette(t);
  return spectrum(t);
}

void main() {
  vec2 uv = (gl_FragCoord.xy - 0.5 * uResolution) / uResolution.y;
  uv /= max(uScale, 0.0001);

  float e = 0.06 + uIntensity * 0.94;
  float env = pow(max(cos(uv.x * PI * 1.3), 0.0), uTaper);

  vec3 col = vec3(0.0);

  for (int i = 0; i < ${MAX_STRANDS}; i++) {
    if (i >= uStrandCount) break;

    float fi = float(i);
    float ph = fi * 1.7 * uSpread;
    float freq = (2.0 + fi * 0.35) * uWaviness;
    float spd = 1.4 + fi * 1.2;

    float tt = uTime * uSpeed;
    float w = sin(uv.x * freq + tt * spd + ph) * 0.60
            + sin(uv.x * freq * 1.1 - tt * spd * 0.7 + ph * 1.7) * 0.40;

    float amp = (0.1 + 0.02 * e) * env * uAmplitude;
    float y = w * amp;

    float d = abs(uv.y - y);
    float thick = (0.001 + 0.05 * e) * (0.35 + env) * uThickness;
    float g = thick / (d + thick * 0.45);
    g = g * g;

    float h = fi / float(uStrandCount) + uv.x * 0.30 + uTime * 0.04 + uHueShift;
    col += strandColor(h) * g * env;
  }

  col *= 0.45 + 0.7 * e;
  col = 1.0 - exp(-col * uGlow);

  float gray = dot(col, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
  col = max(mix(vec3(gray), col, uSaturation), 0.0);

  float lum = max(max(col.r, col.g), col.b);
  float alpha = clamp(lum, 0.0, 1.0) * uOpacity;

  fragColor = vec4(col * uOpacity, alpha);
}
`;

const GLASS_FRAG = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;

uniform sampler2D uScene;
uniform vec2 uResolution;
uniform float uRadius;
uniform float uRefraction;
uniform float uDispersion;

out vec4 fragColor;

vec2 toUv(vec2 p) {
  return p * (uResolution.y / uResolution) + 0.5;
}

void main() {
  vec2 p = (gl_FragCoord.xy - 0.5 * uResolution) / uResolution.y;
  float d = length(p);
  float r = uRadius;

  float edge = fwidth(d) * 1.5;
  float mask = 1.0 - smoothstep(r - edge, r + edge, d);
  if (mask <= 0.0) {
    fragColor = vec4(0.0);
    return;
  }

  // sphere height: 0 at the rim, 1 at the center
  float z = sqrt(max(r * r - d * d, 0.0)) / r;
  float nd = d / r; // 0 at the center, 1 at the rim

  // refraction is confined to a narrow band near the rim; the rest stays undistorted
  vec2 dir = d > 0.0 ? p / d : vec2(0.0);
  float lens = smoothstep(0.85, 1.0, nd) * pow(nd, 6.0);
  vec2 offset = -dir * lens * uRefraction * 0.15;
  vec2 disp = -dir * lens * uDispersion * 0.012;

  vec3 light;
  light.r = texture(uScene, toUv(p + offset - disp)).r;
  light.g = texture(uScene, toUv(p + offset)).g;
  light.b = texture(uScene, toUv(p + offset + disp)).b;

  // neutral fresnel rim (no color tint so the glass stays clear)
  float fres = pow(1.0 - z, 3.0);
  vec3 rim = vec3(1.0) * fres * 0.18;

  // specular highlight from the upper-left
  vec2 lightDir = normalize(vec2(-0.55, 0.6));
  float spec = pow(max(dot(p / max(r, 1e-4), lightDir), 0.0), 6.0);
  spec *= smoothstep(r, r * 0.55, d);

  vec3 emissive = light + rim + vec3(spec) * 0.4;
  float emissiveA = clamp(max(max(emissive.r, emissive.g), emissive.b), 0.0, 1.0);

  // almost clear glass body: only a faint neutral darkening, mostly near the rim
  float bodyA = 0.05 + fres * 0.05;

  // composite emissive light over the clear body (premultiplied)
  float outA = emissiveA + bodyA * (1.0 - emissiveA);
  vec3 outRGB = emissive;

  outRGB *= mask;
  outA *= mask;

  fragColor = vec4(outRGB, outA);
}
`;

const buildPalette = colors => {
  const filled = colors && colors.length ? colors : ['#ffffff'];
  const padded = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < MAX_COLORS; i++) {
    const hex = filled[i] ?? filled[filled.length - 1];
    const c = new Color(hex);
    padded.push([c.r, c.g, c.b]);
  }
  return padded;
};

export default function Strands({
  colors = ['#FF4242', '#7C3AED', '#06B6D4', '#EAB308'],
  count = 3,
  speed = 0.5,
  amplitude = 1,
  waviness = 1,
  thickness = 0.7,
  glow = 2.6,
  taper = 3,
  spread = 1,
  hueShift = 0,
  intensity = 0.6,
  saturation = 1.5,
  opacity = 1,
  scale = 1.5,
  glass = false,
  refraction = 1,
  dispersion = 1,
  glassSize = 1,
  className = '',
  style
}) {
  const propsRef = useRef({});
  propsRef.current = {
    colors,
    count,
    speed,
    amplitude,
    waviness,
    thickness,
    glow,
    taper,
    spread,
    hueShift,
    intensity,
    saturation,
    opacity,
    scale,
    glass,
    refraction,
    dispersion,
    glassSize
  };

  const ctnDom = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const ctn = ctnDom.current;
    if (!ctn) return;

    const renderer = new Renderer({
      alpha: true,
      premultipliedAlpha: true,
      antialias: true
    });
    const gl = renderer.gl;
    gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
    gl.enable(gl.BLEND);
    gl.blendFunc(gl.ONE, gl.ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);
    gl.canvas.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent';

    const geometry = new Triangle(gl);
    if (geometry.attributes.uv) {
      delete geometry.attributes.uv;
    }

    const program = new Program(gl, {
      vertex: VERT,
      fragment: FRAG,
      uniforms: {
        uTime: { value: 0 },
        uResolution: { value: [ctn.offsetWidth, ctn.offsetHeight] },
        uColors: { value: buildPalette(propsRef.current.colors) },
        uColorCount: { value: Math.min(propsRef.current.colors.length, MAX_COLORS) },
        uStrandCount: { value: Math.min(propsRef.current.count, MAX_STRANDS) },
        uSpeed: { value: speed },
        uAmplitude: { value: amplitude },
        uWaviness: { value: waviness },
        uThickness: { value: thickness },
        uGlow: { value: glow },
        uTaper: { value: taper },
        uSpread: { value: spread },
        uHueShift: { value: hueShift },
        uIntensity: { value: intensity },
        uOpacity: { value: opacity },
        uScale: { value: scale },
        uSaturation: { value: saturation }
      }
    });

    const mesh = new Mesh(gl, { geometry, program });

    const renderTarget = new RenderTarget(gl, {
      width: ctn.offsetWidth,
      height: ctn.offsetHeight
    });

    const glassProgram = new Program(gl, {
      vertex: VERT,
      fragment: GLASS_FRAG,
      uniforms: {
        uScene: { value: renderTarget.texture },
        uResolution: { value: [ctn.offsetWidth, ctn.offsetHeight] },
        uRadius: { value: 0.46 * glassSize },
        uRefraction: { value: refraction },
        uDispersion: { value: dispersion }
      }
    });
    const glassMesh = new Mesh(gl, { geometry, program: glassProgram });

    ctn.appendChild(gl.canvas);

    function resize() {
      if (!ctn) return;
      const width = ctn.offsetWidth;
      const height = ctn.offsetHeight;
      renderer.setSize(width, height);
      program.uniforms.uResolution.value = [width, height];
      renderTarget.setSize(width, height);
      glassProgram.uniforms.uResolution.value = [width, height];
    }
    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
    resize();

    let animateId = 0;
    const update = t => {
      animateId = requestAnimationFrame(update);
      const current = propsRef.current;
      program.uniforms.uTime.value = t * 0.001;
      program.uniforms.uColors.value = buildPalette(current.colors);
      program.uniforms.uColorCount.value = Math.min(current.colors.length, MAX_COLORS);
      program.uniforms.uStrandCount.value = Math.min(Math.max(Math.round(current.count), 1), MAX_STRANDS);
      program.uniforms.uSpeed.value = current.speed;
      program.uniforms.uAmplitude.value = current.amplitude;
      program.uniforms.uWaviness.value = current.waviness;
      program.uniforms.uThickness.value = current.thickness;
      program.uniforms.uGlow.value = current.glow;
      program.uniforms.uTaper.value = current.taper;
      program.uniforms.uSpread.value = current.spread;
      program.uniforms.uHueShift.value = current.hueShift;
      program.uniforms.uIntensity.value = current.intensity;
      program.uniforms.uOpacity.value = current.opacity;
      program.uniforms.uScale.value = current.scale;
      program.uniforms.uSaturation.value = current.saturation;

      if (current.glass) {
        renderer.render({ scene: mesh, target: renderTarget });
        glassProgram.uniforms.uScene.value = renderTarget.texture;
        glassProgram.uniforms.uRefraction.value = current.refraction;
        glassProgram.uniforms.uDispersion.value = current.dispersion;
        glassProgram.uniforms.uRadius.value = 0.46 * current.glassSize;
        renderer.render({ scene: glassMesh });
      } else {
        renderer.render({ scene: mesh });
      }
    };
    animateId = requestAnimationFrame(update);

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(animateId);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
      if (ctn && gl.canvas.parentNode === ctn) {
        ctn.removeChild(gl.canvas);
      }
      gl.getExtension('WEBGL_lose_context')?.loseContext();
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  return <div ref={ctnDom} className={`strands-container ${className}`} style={style} />;
}

实现这个效果  ## Integrate the <Strands /> component from React Bits

You are helping integrate an open-source React component into an existing application.

### Component: Strands
### Variant: JavaScript + CSS
### Dependencies: ogl

---

### Usage Example
```jsx
import Strands from './Strands';

<div style={{ width: '100%', height: '600px', position: 'relative' }}>
  <Strands
    colors={["#F97316","#7C3AED","#06B6D4"]}
    count={3}
    speed={0.5}
    amplitude={1}
    waviness={1}
    thickness={0.7}
    glow={2.6}
    taper={3}
    spread={1}
    intensity={0.6}
    saturation={1.5}
    opacity={1}
    scale={1.5}
    glass={false}
    refraction={1}
    dispersion={1}
    glassSize={1}
  />
</div>
```

### Props
| Prop | Type | Default | Description |
|------|------|---------|-------------|
| colors | string[] | ["#FF4242", "#7C3AED", "#06B6D4", "#EAB308"] | Palette of hex colors cycled across the strands. Pass an empty array to use the built-in rainbow spectrum. |
| count | number | 3 | Number of strands woven through the animation. |
| speed | number | 0.5 | How quickly the strands ripple and flow. |
| amplitude | number | 1 | Vertical reach of each strand as it waves up and down. |
| waviness | number | 1 | Density of the curves along each strand. |
| thickness | number | 0.7 | Width of each glowing strand. |
| glow | number | 2.6 | Strength of the luminous bloom around the strands. |
| taper | number | 3 | How sharply the strands fade out toward the edges. |
| spread | number | 1 | Separation between strands so they fan out instead of overlapping. |
| hueShift | number | 0 | Rotates the colors around the strands for variation. |
| intensity | number | 0.6 | Overall brightness and energy of the effect. |
| saturation | number | 1 | Vibrance of the colors. Above 1 makes them more intense, below 1 fades to grayscale. |
| opacity | number | 1 | Overall transparency of the rendered strands. |
| scale | number | 1 | Zooms the whole effect in or out to make the strands bigger or smaller. |
| glass | boolean | false | Renders the strands inside a refractive glass ball. |
| refraction | number | 1 | How strongly the glass ball bends the light passing through it. |
| dispersion | number | 1 | Amount of rainbow color separation along the edges of the glass ball. |
| glassSize | number | 1 | Size of the glass ball relative to the canvas. |

### Full Component Source
```jsx
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Color, Triangle, RenderTarget } from 'ogl';
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

import './Strands.css';

const MAX_STRANDS = 12;
const MAX_COLORS = 8;

const VERT = `#version 300 es
in vec2 position;
void main() {
  gl_Position = vec4(position, 0.0, 1.0);
}
`;

const FRAG = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;

uniform float uTime;
uniform vec2 uResolution;
uniform vec3 uColors[${MAX_COLORS}];
uniform int uColorCount;
uniform int uStrandCount;
uniform float uSpeed;
uniform float uAmplitude;
uniform float uWaviness;
uniform float uThickness;
uniform float uGlow;
uniform float uTaper;
uniform float uSpread;
uniform float uHueShift;
uniform float uIntensity;
uniform float uOpacity;
uniform float uScale;
uniform float uSaturation;

out vec4 fragColor;

const float PI = 3.14159265;

vec3 spectrum(float t) {
  return 0.5 + 0.5 * cos(2.0 * PI * (t + vec3(0.00, 0.33, 0.67)));
}

vec3 samplePalette(float t) {
  t = fract(t);
  float scaled = t * float(uColorCount);
  int idx = int(floor(scaled));
  float blend = fract(scaled);
  int nextIdx = idx + 1;
  if (nextIdx >= uColorCount) nextIdx = 0;
  return mix(uColors[idx], uColors[nextIdx], blend);
}

vec3 strandColor(float t) {
  if (uColorCount > 0) return samplePalette(t);
  return spectrum(t);
}

void main() {
  vec2 uv = (gl_FragCoord.xy - 0.5 * uResolution) / uResolution.y;
  uv /= max(uScale, 0.0001);

  float e = 0.06 + uIntensity * 0.94;
  float env = pow(max(cos(uv.x * PI * 1.3), 0.0), uTaper);

  vec3 col = vec3(0.0);

  for (int i = 0; i < ${MAX_STRANDS}; i++) {
    if (i >= uStrandCount) break;

    float fi = float(i);
    float ph = fi * 1.7 * uSpread;
    float freq = (2.0 + fi * 0.35) * uWaviness;
    float spd = 1.4 + fi * 1.2;

    float tt = uTime * uSpeed;
    float w = sin(uv.x * freq + tt * spd + ph) * 0.60
            + sin(uv.x * freq * 1.1 - tt * spd * 0.7 + ph * 1.7) * 0.40;

    float amp = (0.1 + 0.02 * e) * env * uAmplitude;
    float y = w * amp;

    float d = abs(uv.y - y);
    float thick = (0.001 + 0.05 * e) * (0.35 + env) * uThickness;
    float g = thick / (d + thick * 0.45);
    g = g * g;

    float h = fi / float(uStrandCount) + uv.x * 0.30 + uTime * 0.04 + uHueShift;
    col += strandColor(h) * g * env;
  }

  col *= 0.45 + 0.7 * e;
  col = 1.0 - exp(-col * uGlow);

  float gray = dot(col, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
  col = max(mix(vec3(gray), col, uSaturation), 0.0);

  float lum = max(max(col.r, col.g), col.b);
  float alpha = clamp(lum, 0.0, 1.0) * uOpacity;

  fragColor = vec4(col * uOpacity, alpha);
}
`;

const GLASS_FRAG = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;

uniform sampler2D uScene;
uniform vec2 uResolution;
uniform float uRadius;
uniform float uRefraction;
uniform float uDispersion;

out vec4 fragColor;

vec2 toUv(vec2 p) {
  return p * (uResolution.y / uResolution) + 0.5;
}

void main() {
  vec2 p = (gl_FragCoord.xy - 0.5 * uResolution) / uResolution.y;
  float d = length(p);
  float r = uRadius;

  float edge = fwidth(d) * 1.5;
  float mask = 1.0 - smoothstep(r - edge, r + edge, d);
  if (mask <= 0.0) {
    fragColor = vec4(0.0);
    return;
  }

  // sphere height: 0 at the rim, 1 at the center
  float z = sqrt(max(r * r - d * d, 0.0)) / r;
  float nd = d / r; // 0 at the center, 1 at the rim

  // refraction is confined to a narrow band near the rim; the rest stays undistorted
  vec2 dir = d > 0.0 ? p / d : vec2(0.0);
  float lens = smoothstep(0.85, 1.0, nd) * pow(nd, 6.0);
  vec2 offset = -dir * lens * uRefraction * 0.15;
  vec2 disp = -dir * lens * uDispersion * 0.012;

  vec3 light;
  light.r = texture(uScene, toUv(p + offset - disp)).r;
  light.g = texture(uScene, toUv(p + offset)).g;
  light.b = texture(uScene, toUv(p + offset + disp)).b;

  // neutral fresnel rim (no color tint so the glass stays clear)
  float fres = pow(1.0 - z, 3.0);
  vec3 rim = vec3(1.0) * fres * 0.18;

  // specular highlight from the upper-left
  vec2 lightDir = normalize(vec2(-0.55, 0.6));
  float spec = pow(max(dot(p / max(r, 1e-4), lightDir), 0.0), 6.0);
  spec *= smoothstep(r, r * 0.55, d);

  vec3 emissive = light + rim + vec3(spec) * 0.4;
  float emissiveA = clamp(max(max(emissive.r, emissive.g), emissive.b), 0.0, 1.0);

  // almost clear glass body: only a faint neutral darkening, mostly near the rim
  float bodyA = 0.05 + fres * 0.05;

  // composite emissive light over the clear body (premultiplied)
  float outA = emissiveA + bodyA * (1.0 - emissiveA);
  vec3 outRGB = emissive;

  outRGB *= mask;
  outA *= mask;

  fragColor = vec4(outRGB, outA);
}
`;

const buildPalette = colors => {
  const filled = colors && colors.length ? colors : ['#ffffff'];
  const padded = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < MAX_COLORS; i++) {
    const hex = filled[i] ?? filled[filled.length - 1];
    const c = new Color(hex);
    padded.push([c.r, c.g, c.b]);
  }
  return padded;
};

export default function Strands({
  colors = ['#FF4242', '#7C3AED', '#06B6D4', '#EAB308'],
  count = 3,
  speed = 0.5,
  amplitude = 1,
  waviness = 1,
  thickness = 0.7,
  glow = 2.6,
  taper = 3,
  spread = 1,
  hueShift = 0,
  intensity = 0.6,
  saturation = 1.5,
  opacity = 1,
  scale = 1.5,
  glass = false,
  refraction = 1,
  dispersion = 1,
  glassSize = 1,
  className = '',
  style
}) {
  const propsRef = useRef({});
  propsRef.current = {
    colors,
    count,
    speed,
    amplitude,
    waviness,
    thickness,
    glow,
    taper,
    spread,
    hueShift,
    intensity,
    saturation,
    opacity,
    scale,
    glass,
    refraction,
    dispersion,
    glassSize
  };

  const ctnDom = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const ctn = ctnDom.current;
    if (!ctn) return;

    const renderer = new Renderer({
      alpha: true,
      premultipliedAlpha: true,
      antialias: true
    });
    const gl = renderer.gl;
    gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
    gl.enable(gl.BLEND);
    gl.blendFunc(gl.ONE, gl.ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);
    gl.canvas.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent';

    const geometry = new Triangle(gl);
    if (geometry.attributes.uv) {
      delete geometry.attributes.uv;
    }

    const program = new Program(gl, {
      vertex: VERT,
      fragment: FRAG,
      uniforms: {
        uTime: { value: 0 },
        uResolution: { value: [ctn.offsetWidth, ctn.offsetHeight] },
        uColors: { value: buildPalette(propsRef.current.colors) },
        uColorCount: { value: Math.min(propsRef.current.colors.length, MAX_COLORS) },
        uStrandCount: { value: Math.min(propsRef.current.count, MAX_STRANDS) },
        uSpeed: { value: speed },
        uAmplitude: { value: amplitude },
        uWaviness: { value: waviness },
        uThickness: { value: thickness },
        uGlow: { value: glow },
        uTaper: { value: taper },
        uSpread: { value: spread },
        uHueShift: { value: hueShift },
        uIntensity: { value: intensity },
        uOpacity: { value: opacity },
        uScale: { value: scale },
        uSaturation: { value: saturation }
      }
    });

    const mesh = new Mesh(gl, { geometry, program });

    const renderTarget = new RenderTarget(gl, {
      width: ctn.offsetWidth,
      height: ctn.offsetHeight
    });

    const glassProgram = new Program(gl, {
      vertex: VERT,
      fragment: GLASS_FRAG,
      uniforms: {
        uScene: { value: renderTarget.texture },
        uResolution: { value: [ctn.offsetWidth, ctn.offsetHeight] },
        uRadius: { value: 0.46 * glassSize },
        uRefraction: { value: refraction },
        uDispersion: { value: dispersion }
      }
    });
    const glassMesh = new Mesh(gl, { geometry, program: glassProgram });

    ctn.appendChild(gl.canvas);

    function resize() {
      if (!ctn) return;
      const width = ctn.offsetWidth;
      const height = ctn.offsetHeight;
      renderer.setSize(width, height);
      program.uniforms.uResolution.value = [width, height];
      renderTarget.setSize(width, height);
      glassProgram.uniforms.uResolution.value = [width, height];
    }
    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
    resize();

    let animateId = 0;
    const update = t => {
      animateId = requestAnimationFrame(update);
      const current = propsRef.current;
      program.uniforms.uTime.value = t * 0.001;
      program.uniforms.uColors.value = buildPalette(current.colors);
      program.uniforms.uColorCount.value = Math.min(current.colors.length, MAX_COLORS);
      program.uniforms.uStrandCount.value = Math.min(Math.max(Math.round(current.count), 1), MAX_STRANDS);
      program.uniforms.uSpeed.value = current.speed;
      program.uniforms.uAmplitude.value = current.amplitude;
      program.uniforms.uWaviness.value = current.waviness;
      program.uniforms.uThickness.value = current.thickness;
      program.uniforms.uGlow.value = current.glow;
      program.uniforms.uTaper.value = current.taper;
      program.uniforms.uSpread.value = current.spread;
      program.uniforms.uHueShift.value = current.hueShift;
      program.uniforms.uIntensity.value = current.intensity;
      program.uniforms.uOpacity.value = current.opacity;
      program.uniforms.uScale.value = current.scale;
      program.uniforms.uSaturation.value = current.saturation;

      if (current.glass) {
        renderer.render({ scene: mesh, target: renderTarget });
        glassProgram.uniforms.uScene.value = renderTarget.texture;
        glassProgram.uniforms.uRefraction.value = current.refraction;
        glassProgram.uniforms.uDispersion.value = current.dispersion;
        glassProgram.uniforms.uRadius.value = 0.46 * current.glassSize;
        renderer.render({ scene: glassMesh });
      } else {
        renderer.render({ scene: mesh });
      }
    };
    animateId = requestAnimationFrame(update);

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(animateId);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
      if (ctn && gl.canvas.parentNode === ctn) {
        ctn.removeChild(gl.canvas);
      }
      gl.getExtension('WEBGL_lose_context')?.loseContext();
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  return <div ref={ctnDom} className={`strands-container ${className}`} style={style} />;
}
